- Compositor (stage 2 task 11 review, controller ruling): a desktop switch focuses the new desktop's top window, as Openbox does on X11. Before, after a pager click to another desktop and back, the keyboard stayed on the panel and no window was active until the user clicked one. Now `switch_desktop` (IPC `desktops/switch` and the ext-workspace `activate`) and the switch inside `activate_window` (when the activated window is not shown yet) call `_focus_desktop_top`: the topmost shown window gets the keyboard and becomes the active window; under an exclusive keyboard layer it only becomes the active window and the layer keeps the keyboard. A switch to the current desktop changes nothing; a desktop with no window leaves the focus as it was. Also: the `WindowView.activated` doc comment now says "the active window", not "the keyboard". Tests (headless): `a_desktop_switch_away_and_back_gives_the_top_window_the_keyboard`, `a_desktop_switch_activates_the_top_window_of_the_new_desktop`, `a_desktop_switch_under_an_exclusive_layer_only_changes_the_active_window` (RED: 3 failed; GREEN), plus the guards `a_switch_to_the_current_desktop_changes_nothing` and `the_active_window_destroyed_while_the_panel_has_the_keyboard_leaves_no_active_window` (passed before and after). `cargo test -p opusdm-compositor`: 108 unit, 77 headless, 15 ipc (1 ignored, as before); headless + ipc 5 runs green; clippy `-D warnings` clean. Sandbox (run-native.sh, private Xvfb :7, config copy, `~/.config/opusdm` sha256 and picom.conf mtime unchanged, built with the panel fix above): pager click to desktop 2 and back, then the Lister is active in the tasklist and pager and Backspace reaches it with no click (it went to the parent folder): PASS; tasklist minimize 3/3, restore 3/3: PASS. NOT CHECKED: x11 / labwc (crates/compositor only); the ext-workspace path in a live sandbox (headless tests go through the IPC path, which calls the same `switch_desktop`).
- Fix (compositor stage 2 task 11 review): the panel sends window actions to opusdm-compositor's IPC only when its window list comes from there. New per-process decision `wl_windows::native_active()` = `under_native()` AND the native watch started, set once by `spawn` (`NATIVE_ACTIVE`, a `OnceLock`). Before, after a fallback to foreign-toplevel (watch failed while the socket existed) the actions and the tasklist "Move to desktop" menu still went to the native IPC with foreign-toplevel ids. Both id spaces count from 1, so a tasklist click or Close Window could act on a different real window. Now a fallback logs `err_log!` and keeps every action on the Wayland path for the rest of the process. An action before `spawn` decided (it cannot happen today: `spawn` runs synchronously before the GTK loop) is treated as not native and logged. `activate_window`, `iconify_window`, `close_window`, `move_window_to_desktop`, `switch_desktop`, `kill_window` and the tasklist submenu use `native_active()`; X11 and labwc are unchanged (`under_native()` false, nothing logged). Test: `native_actions_need_the_compositor_and_a_started_watch` (the pure decision). `cargo test -p opusdm-panel`: 199 passed; clippy: no new warning (the 3 known opusdm-wayland `chunks_exact` errors remain). The native worker duplicating the Wayfire worker is noted in the new `OPTIMIZE.md` (kept: the owner's Wayfire session runs that code). NOT verified: the fallback path itself in a live session (a compositor that accepts connections but refuses `watch` was not simulated).
- Compositor (stage 2 task 11 scope extension, controller ruling): a layer surface never takes the active window. `State.active_window: Option<WindowId>` is the last toplevel that took the keyboard; `focus_window(Some(w))` (outside an exclusive layer) sets it, `focus_window(None)` clears it. `focus_layer` (a panel click on an `OnDemand` layer through `_click_layer`, or an exclusive layer such as the Run window) no longer takes `Activated` from every window: `apply_active_window` keeps it on the active window and takes it from the others (a window mapped under an exclusive layer still does not become active). The id is cleared (`forget_active_window`) when its window is hidden (`_hide`: minimized, other desktop), unmapped by a null buffer, destroyed, or found dead by `refresh`; then the existing focus logic decides the next window (when the keyboard is on the hidden window the topmost shown window gets it and becomes active; when it is on an OnDemand layer it stays there and no window is active until one is focused). The xdg `Activated` state stays the single source, so `windows/list`, the watch events and the foreign-toplevel states all follow. New headless-only IPC method `debug/click {x, y}` (pointer motion + left press/release through the real input path) for the tests. Tests: 5 headless tests (`a_click_on_an_on_demand_layer_keeps_the_window_active`, `an_exclusive_layer_keeps_the_window_active`, `a_minimized_active_window_is_no_longer_active_and_the_next_one_is`, `a_window_minimized_while_the_panel_has_the_keyboard_is_no_longer_active`, `focusing_another_window_after_a_panel_click_makes_only_it_active`), RED 4 failed / 1 passed (the guard), GREEN 5 passed; 1 unit test `debug_click_needs_test_input`. `cargo test -p opusdm-compositor`: 108 unit, 72 headless, 15 ipc (1 ignored, as before) passed; headless + ipc 5 runs in a row green; `cargo clippy -p opusdm-compositor --all-targets -- -D warnings` clean. Sandbox (run-native.sh on a private Xvfb :7, config copy, `~/.config/opusdm` sha256 and picom.conf mtime unchanged): tasklist minimize of the active Lister 6/6, restore 6/6, instant press-release 4/4; Lister Backspace and Alt+Left after panel clicks PASS. NOT CHECKED: the hub's Run window (exclusive layer) in the live sandbox (covered by the headless test only), x11 / labwc (the change is in crates/compositor only, which they do not run).
- Panel (compositor stage 2 task 11): native branch of the tasklist, pager and desktops under opusdm-compositor. `wl_windows::under_native()` reads `opusdm_wayland::native::under_native()` once per process (a `OnceLock`), so the compositor removing its socket at exit cannot flip the panel to the labwc / foreign-toplevel paths mid-shutdown. `spawn` tries `spawn_native` first (falls back to foreign-toplevel with an `err_log!` when `native::watch` fails); `native_events` mirrors `wayfire_events`: `Desktops { count, current, names "1".."N" }`, `Clients` with the real 0-based desktop (never -1), pid, app id, title and the geometry as `WinRect`, and `ActiveWindow` (the `activated` window). `switch_desktop`, `activate_window`, `iconify_window`, `close_window` and `move_window_to_desktop` take the native IPC first under native, on a worker thread of their own (`opusdm-native-call`, click order kept, each failure logged as `compositor: <what> failed: ...`). The tasklist "Move to desktop" submenu shows when `is_x11() || under_native()`. Force kill under native calls `native::kill(id)` (the compositor kills with its own pid guards); elsewhere it still kills the pid with `kill(2)`. When the compositor goes away the watch threads stop with a log line each and the panel keeps its last state (no reconnection, by design). On X11, labwc and Wayfire nothing new runs: the only new step is the one env-var check behind `under_native()`, which is false there. Tests: 4 new `native_events` unit tests (desktop, pid and rect carried; a window on desktop 2 is `desktop: 2`; names "1".."4"; only what changed is sent; empty app id and negative size). `cargo test -p opusdm-panel`: 198 passed. Clippy: no new warning in the panel (the 3 known `chunks_exact` errors of opusdm-wayland still block a plain `-D warnings` run). Manual checks (Xvfb :7, private configs, `~/.config/opusdm` sha256 + picom.conf mtime unchanged): run-native.sh: PASS - the Lister on desktop 1 shows only in pager box 1 with its real rectangle; pager clicks switch desktops; tasklist Move to desktop (names "1".."4") moves a scratch foot to desktop 2; Force kill of that foot (`window/kill`, SIGKILL to its pid, gone from tasklist and pager); restore from the tasklist; Lister Enter in the path bar, double click, Backspace, Alt+Left/Right, F4. Tasklist "click the active window to minimize" was FAIL (flaky, about 1 click in 3) at first: the panel bar is an `OnDemand` keyboard layer, the compositor's click focus gave it the keyboard on the press and took `activated` from the window, so the button often activated instead of minimizing (labwc 4/4 and X11 3/3 were deterministic). Fixed in the compositor by the next commit ("a layer surface never takes the active window", entry above): now PASS under native, 6/6 minimizes and 6/6 restores with plain clicks, plus 4/4 instant press-release toggles. Also under native the first panel click right after a tasklist popover menu closed was swallowed twice (no request sent); X11 did not show it. run-x11.sh (Xephyr :8): PASS - panel looks as on the base commit; minimize/restore, Move to desktop ("desktop 1".."desktop 4", `_NET_WM_DESKTOP` = 1), pager switch, Lister Enter / double click / Backspace / Alt+Left/Right, hotkeys Alt+1/Alt+2, Ctrl+Shift+space, Ctrl+Print. FAIL, pre-existing (same on the base commit 681dc81 built from a `git archive` export): Ctrl+backslash starts no terminal (likely the sandbox's Italian keymap) and Lister F4 ends the Lister process with no log line (also on labwc; F4 works under native with a US keymap; the opusdm-lister binary is not touched by this change). run-wayland.sh (labwc): PASS - panel looks as on the base commit (placeholder boxes on every desktop), menu still only "Close Window", minimize/restore, pager switch, Lister double click / Backspace, hotkeys Ctrl+Shift+space, Alt+1/Alt+2, Ctrl+Print; Ctrl+backslash FAIL as on x11. NOT CHECKED: global hotkeys under native (Task 12), Wayfire (no live run), pager drag-and-drop under native, middle-click close and "Close Window" under native, a compositor restart while the panel runs.
- Fix (compositor stage 2 task 10 review, round 2): `opusdm_wayland::native` hotkey writer. (1) A failed `hotkeys/set` write by the writer thread (a 2 s `SO_SNDTIMEO` timeout on a stalled compositor, EPIPE, or a partial frame) now shuts the hub connection down (`Shutdown::Both`, and a failed shutdown is logged too). Before, the stale connection stayed alive and the new table was never applied until some unrelated reconnection, or a half frame desynced the stream. Now the hotkey thread sees the loss, logs it, reconnects after `RECONNECT_DELAY` and sends the stored table, which is always the latest. A failed resend inside the handshake already returned `Err`, which drops that stream. (2) `watch_hotkeys` spawns the writer thread first: if the hotkey thread then fails to start, the `Err` drops the only sender and the writer thread ends, so `Err` means nothing keeps running and a retrying caller cannot start a second hub. (3) The cross-reference in the Task 10 entry now points to the fix entries above it. Test: `a_failed_table_write_forces_a_reconnection_that_sends_it` (the fake server shuts down its read side, `set()` hits EPIPE, the next connection must carry the new table). It is RED without the shutdown (no reconnection: "no client" after 5 s) and GREEN with it. `cargo test -p opusdm-wayland`: 39 passed (3 runs). Clippy: same 3 pre-existing errors, none new. NOT verified: the timeout path (as opposed to EPIPE) against a really stalled compositor; it runs the same code after `send` returns `Err`. The case where the hotkey thread fails to spawn was only checked by reading the code.
- Fix (compositor stage 2 task 10 review): `opusdm_wayland::native::HotkeyWatch::set` can no longer block its caller (the hub's owner thread, Task 12) on a slow compositor. Before, the handshake held the shared lock across up to 4 socket operations with a 2 s timeout each, and `set()` waited on that lock and wrote to the socket itself. Now `set()` only does `tx.send(table)` on an mpsc channel. A new `opusdm-native-hotkey-writer` thread stores the table (latest of a burst), bumps a generation and writes `hotkeys/set` on the live connection. The handshake copies (generation, table) under a short lock, does all its I/O with no lock held, then publishes its writer and resends the latest table if the generation moved in between, so a new table is never lost. A refused handshake `hotkeys/set` is now commented and logged as "this process may not be the hub and hub hotkeys are swallowed". The retry log is bounded: the exact "compositor IPC lost: {e}, retrying" line on the first failure, then "still lost after N attempts" every 30 attempts (about once a minute), and "compositor IPC back after N failed attempts" on reconnection. Tests: new `set_never_waits_for_a_slow_handshake_and_its_table_is_still_sent` (RED on the old code: "set() blocked for 2.01s"; GREEN now); the set test now asserts that the `errors` reply never reaches `on_hit`. `cargo test -p opusdm-wayland`: 38 passed (3 runs). Clippy: the same 3 pre-existing errors, none new. The headless smoke run was repeated and passed: both hotkeys, set, requests, and a single loss line after the kill. NOT verified: the "still lost" line (needs 60 s of downtime) and the "back" line (needs a compositor restart on the same socket); both are covered only by reading the code.
- Feature (compositor stage 2 task 10): `opusdm_wayland::native`, the client of the opusdm-compositor private IPC (used next by the panel, Task 11, and the hub, Task 12; nothing calls it yet, so the X11, labwc and Wayfire sessions are untouched). New `crates/wayland/src/frame.rs`: the Wayfire framing moved out of `wayfire.rs` as a pure move (`frame`, `send`, `receive(stream, max)`, plus `receive_from(stream, max, peer)` so Wayfire keeps its exact "bad JSON from Wayfire" error; same 16 MiB Wayfire limit, same 2 s timeout, same errors; every `wayfire.rs` test unchanged and green). `native.rs`: `ENV_SOCKET = "OPUSDM_COMPOSITOR_SOCKET"`, `socket_path()`, `under_native()` (variable set and the socket exists), `NativeWindow`, `NativeState { windows, count, current }` with `read()` and `active()`, `Client` / `call`, the requests `activate`, `set_minimized`, `close`, `kill`, `move_to_desktop`, `switch_desktop`, `set_desktop_count -> u32` (the clamped count), `watch(on_change)` (same shape as `wayfire::watch`: event thread + state thread, bursts merged, routes on the `"event"` key, stops with an `err_log!` line when the compositor goes away), and `watch_hotkeys(table, on_hit)`: one detached thread sends `hotkeys/set` then `watch` on the same connection, calls `on_hit(function)` per `hotkey` event, logs `compositor IPC lost: {e}, retrying` and reconnects every `RECONNECT_DELAY` (2 s), sending the table again; never exits. Replies are capped at 4 MiB (the server's `IPC_MAX_PENDING`). **Signature change from the brief:** `watch_hotkeys` returns `Result<HotkeyWatch, String>` instead of `Result<(), String>`; `HotkeyWatch::set(table)` hands the new table to a writer thread (used on every reconnection, sent as `hotkeys/set` on the live connection; the reply, with its per-key `errors`, is logged by the hotkey thread). See the two review fix entries above this one: `set` never blocks, and a failed write forces a reconnection. Dropping the handle does not stop the thread. `Err` from `watch_hotkeys` only when the socket is missing or the thread cannot start; a compositor not answering yet goes into the retry loop. Verified: `cargo test -p opusdm-wayland` (37 passed, 6 new in `native`, 3 in `frame`), clippy with no new warnings (the 3 pre-existing `chunks_exact_to_as_chunks` errors in model.rs / screencopy.rs left as ruled; clean with that lint allowed), and a smoke run of a scratch client against `opusdm-compositor --headless` (own XDG_RUNTIME_DIR in /tmp) with foot as a client: read, watch, both hotkeys (before and after `set`), set-count (6, and 99 clamped to 16), switch, minimize, activate, move-to-desktop, close, an error reply, and after killing the compositor the hotkey thread logged a retry every 2 s and the watch threads stopped with a log line. NOT verified: `kill()` against the real compositor (not run; same `call` path as the other requests); the panel/hub using this module (Tasks 11/12); the OpusDM session itself (nothing in it calls this code yet).
- Feature (compositor stage 2 task 9): the window menu is drawn in the nested backend. New `src/menu/render.rs`: `render_menu(menu, theme, scale) -> Result<MemoryRenderBuffer, String>` (and `render_submenu`) draw with cairo + pango (`Sans 10`) into an ARGB8888 buffer: border colour, one vertical `menu.items.bg` gradient (`color` -> `colorTo`) over the inner box as Openbox does, the `menu.items.active.bg` gradient behind the selected item (the parent stays highlighted while its submenu is open), labels left-aligned at `border_width + padding` (ellipsized), a `>` on the submenu parent, disabled text colour for disabled items, a 1 px line for the separator. The border is drawn INSIDE the `MENU_WIDTH` x items-height box of the Task 8 geometry, so the image and the hit test use the same rectangles and `menu_size` / `place_menu` are unchanged (the first and last item lose 4 px of visible height to the border). `WindowMenu::images` caches the menu and submenu images keyed by the `MenuNav` state and the scale: drawn again only when the selection, the submenu or the items change; an error empties the cache and is returned. `src/backend/nested.rs`: new `NestedElement` (`render_elements!`: surfaces + `MemoryRenderBufferRenderElement`); the submenu and the menu are drawn above OVERLAY and below the client cursor at the `WindowMenu::layout()` boxes; a draw or texture error is logged through the stage 1 `ErrorThrottle` (at most once per second per kind: "window menu render", "window menu texture") and the frame goes on without the menu, which stays open and usable by keyboard. `MenuTheme` gets `border` (`menu.border.color`, else `window.active.border.color`, default `#181818`, as Openbox); the `#[allow(dead_code)]` on `Rgb`, `MenuTheme` and `MenuLayout` are gone. New deps: `cairo-rs` 0.21 and `pangocairo` 0.21 (0.21.5, same as gtk4's; `cargo tree -d` shows no second pango / cairo / glib). The headless backend is unchanged (no renderer).
  - Verified: 6 new unit tests (`render_menu` gives `MENU_WIDTH` x `5 * MENU_ITEM_HEIGHT + MENU_SEPARATOR_HEIGHT` bytes at scale 1 and 2 (failed first against a stub), border pixel and active/normal item colours, submenu only while open (4 desktops -> 4 items), the cache draws again only on state or scale change, scale 0 is an error and caches nothing; the `menu.border.color` precedence); crate tests (107 unit, 67 headless, 15 ipc), clippy `--all-targets -D warnings`, headless + ipc 5 times. Nested backend on a private `Xvfb :7` (not Xephyr, controller ruling; `XDG_RUNTIME_DIR` in a short `/tmp/t9rt.*` dir because the scratchpad path is longer than `SUN_LEN`; scratch `compositor.json` `{"window_menu": "Super+Alt+space"}`; `--startup foot`; xdotool on :7 only):
    - Super+Alt+space opens the menu, drawn at the window corner (Maximize highlighted blue, Minimize, Move to desktop with `>`, separator, Close, Kill): PASS (`task9-01-menu-open.png`).
    - Down Down Right: "Move to desktop" highlighted, submenu 1-4 drawn at its right at the item's top, "1" (own desktop) selected in disabled colour: PASS (`task9-02-submenu.png`).
    - Escape closes the menu (log "window menu closed: Escape"): PASS.
    - Reopen, Down, Return: foot minimized (log "window 1 minimized: true"), nothing left on screen: PASS (`task9-03-after-minimize.png`).
    - No "window menu render" / "window menu texture" error in the log: PASS. `~/.config/opusdm` sha256 and picom.conf mtime unchanged.

- Fix (compositor stage 2 task 8 review): while the window menu is open a hub hotkey is swallowed with `err_log!("hotkey {combo}: swallowed, the window menu is open ...")` (it was dropped with a debug line only, rule 3) and the hub gets no event; a second window menu key (Alt+Space) closes the menu (toggle, as Openbox; `run_binding`); the key filter runs `check_window_menu` before routing a press, so a menu whose window went since the last frame tick cannot eat a key (the then-unreachable "key dropped" branch of `window_menu_key` now logs with `err_log!`); scroll (`pointer_axis`) is dropped while the menu is open (motion still reaches clients); `kill_window_pid` takes the source for its log line (`window/kill` keeps its exact line, the menu logs "window menu Kill"); the client-death menu test uses `std::mem::forget` on the window so it really tests a client death, not an `xdg_toplevel.destroy`.
  - Verified: 2 new headless tests (hub hotkey swallowed and logged, no hub event, menu still open; second Alt+Space closes the menu and Return then reaches the client) failed before the fix and pass now; crate tests (101 unit, 67 headless, 15 ipc), clippy `-D warnings`, headless + ipc 5 times.

- Feature (compositor stage 2 task 8): the window menu (Alt+Space), model and input only, no drawing yet (task 9). New `src/menu/` (`mod.rs` geometry + `State` side, `model.rs` items and `MenuNav`, `theme.rs` OpusDMTheme colours). `State::open_window_menu` (replaces the task 5 stub) opens on the window with the keyboard (the root of a focused popup), at its top-left corner kept inside the output; no shown window with the keyboard (nothing, a layer, an exclusive layer) -> `debug_log!`, nothing opens. Items: Maximize / Restore, Minimize, Move to desktop ("1".."N", own desktop disabled; the parent disabled with one desktop), separator, Close, Kill. Keys while open (`src/input.rs`): every non-modifier press goes to the menu (Up / Down skip separators and disabled items and wrap, Right / Enter open the submenu on its first item, Left closes it, Enter runs, Escape closes) and its keycode is recorded in `swallowed_keys`, so no press or release reaches a client; modifiers still go to the client (no stuck modifier, xkb state untouched); only the quit binding still matches (Alt+Space again or hub hotkeys are swallowed by the menu). Pointer: a press while open goes to `window_menu_click` (hit test on fixed metrics `MENU_ITEM_HEIGHT` 24, `MENU_WIDTH` 200, `MENU_SEPARATOR_HEIGHT` 9; inside selects, outside closes) and its release is swallowed (`swallowed_buttons`). A client popup grab (open client menu) is ended first (`dismiss_popups_and_grabs`, as task 6 does for an exclusive layer). The menu closes with no action when its window is destroyed or its client dies (`toplevel_destroyed`), is hidden or gone (`check_window_menu` from `refresh` and before every menu key / click), or an exclusive keyboard layer maps (`src/shell/layer.rs`). Actions look the window up by `WindowId` when they run (unknown -> `err_log!`, nothing): maximize goes through the stage 1 `maximize_request` / `unmaximize_request` path. `window/kill` factored into `State::kill_window` (and `kill_window_pid`, which the IPC reply uses; behaviour unchanged). `MenuTheme::load` reads `OpusDMTheme/openbox-3/themerc` from `$XDG_DATA_HOME/themes` (default `~/.local/share/themes`), `~/.themes`, `/usr/share/themes` once at startup; not found -> defaults (equal to OpusDMTheme) + one `err_log!`.
  - Verified: 24 new unit tests (items, navigation incl. "Down from Move to desktop skips the separator and lands on Close" and "Up from the first item wraps to Kill", clicks, geometry and hit test, theme parse of the repo themerc with `active_bg.0` = `#6d95de`, theme dir search) and 9 new headless tests (minimize, window goes, client dies, maximize toggle, move to desktop through the submenu, Escape, exclusive layer closes it, no focused window, client menu ended first); crate tests (101 unit, 65 headless, 15 ipc), clippy `-D warnings`, headless + ipc 5 times. Nested backend on a private Xvfb (:97, weston-terminal, xdotool to the nested window only): Alt+Space Down Return minimized the window, Alt+Space Return maximized it, Alt+Space Escape closed the menu, every release swallowed (log); the theme was read from `/usr/share/themes/OpusDMTheme`. Owner config hash and picom.conf mtime unchanged.

- Feature (compositor stage 2 task 7): xdg-activation (`xdg_activation_v1`, Smithay `wayland::xdg_activation`, no new Smithay feature), new `src/activation.rs`. `token_created` accepts every token (`debug_log!` with the app id) after pruning tokens older than `ACTIVATION_TOKEN_TIMEOUT` (10 s) with `retain_tokens`, so unused tokens do not pile up. `request_activation` removes the token in every case; a token younger than 10 s on a mapped toplevel calls `activate_window` (desktop switch, restore, raise, focus; an exclusive keyboard layer keeps the keyboard as before); on a toplevel not mapped yet it sets `WindowInfo::pending_activation`, applied by `handle_commit` right after the map (`debug_log!` "pending activation of window N applied") and dropped with the window (`toplevel_destroyed` logs "discarded"); an expired token or a surface that is not a toplevel is refused with `err_log!`. Smithay 0.7.0 ignores an `activate` with an unknown token silently, so `xdg_activation_v1` has its own `Dispatch` wrapper (as the layer-shell one) that logs "activation of ... refused: unknown or expired token" and then hands the request to Smithay. Harness: `TestClient::activation_token`, `request_activation`, `map_toplevel_activated`, `activate_toplevel_destroyed_before_map`, `Compositor::start_debug` (sets `OPUSDM_DEBUG`).
  - Verified: 6 new headless tests (valid token focuses, restores a window on another desktop, unknown token changes nothing and logs, token single use, activation before map applied on map, pending activation discarded with its window) + 1 unit test (freshness bound); crate tests (77 unit, 56 headless, 15 ipc), clippy `-D warnings`, headless + ipc suites 5 times green. Nested on a private Xvfb :7 (own pids, temp HOME/XDG_CONFIG_HOME): a real GTK4 client (`gtk4-demo`, `XDG_ACTIVATION_TOKEN=not-a-token`) binds the global and sends `activate`, the compositor logs the refusal and the window maps normally; foot unaffected. `~/.config/opusdm` checksums and picom.conf mtime unchanged.
  - NOT verified: a valid token from a real client (launcher or a GTK notification click: the Task 13 exit check), the expired-token path end to end (only the 10 s bound by unit test; no test waits 10 s), a token with a serial check (no serial or focus policy is applied: any client with a fresh token may activate), the live session.
- Fix (compositor stage 2 task 6, review): `_end_popup_grabs_for` takes the grab root from the keyboard grab only. The pointer grab start focus was also used, but a pointer grab may be a button hold (ClickGrab) or a window move (MoveGrab), which an exclusive layer must not end; a popup grab always sets both grabs and `dismiss_popups_and_grabs` unsets the pointer grab on that root. New test `a_layer_that_becomes_exclusive_over_an_open_menu_closes_it` (layer committed non-exclusive, menu opened, layer switches to Exclusive: popup_done and the layer has the keyboard); harness `set_layer_keyboard`. Verified: crate tests (76 unit, 50 headless, 15 ipc), clippy `-D warnings`, headless 5 runs. NOT verified: a window drag or held button while a layer maps (no pointer injection in the harness; by code review the pointer grab is no longer read); nothing run nested or live.
- Feature (compositor stage 2 task 6): an exclusive keyboard layer (TOP / OVERLAY) that maps over an open menu ends the popup grab first. In `handle_commit` (`src/shell/layer.rs`), right before the layer becomes `exclusive_layer` (covers a new layer and an existing layer that becomes exclusive on a later commit), `_end_popup_grabs_for` takes the roots of the keyboard and pointer grabs (grab start focus) and calls `State::dismiss_popups_and_grabs(root)` on each (popup_done, grabs unset, `debug_log!`), then the layer takes the keyboard. A grab rooted at the layer itself (its own menu) is left alone; no grab: unchanged. Test `an_exclusive_layer_mapped_over_an_open_menu_keeps_the_keyboard` (failed before: no popup_done).
  - Verified: crate tests (76 unit, 49 headless, 15 ipc), clippy `-D warnings`, headless suite 5 times.
  - NOT verified: the pointer grab side is not asserted; nothing run in the nested backend or the owner session. (Corrected in task 13: the "becomes exclusive on a later commit" path is tested since the task 6 review fix, `a_layer_that_becomes_exclusive_over_an_open_menu_closes_it`.)
- Fix (compositor stage 2 task 5 review): hotkey events go to the hub only. The hub is the connection that sent `hotkeys/set` and is a `watch` stream (`hotkeys/set` then `watch` on the same connection, or `hotkeys/set` on a watch stream); a `hotkeys/set` from a connection that is not watching replaces the table and makes it the candidate, which becomes the hub when it sends `watch`, and a candidate that closes first is a one-shot client (`debug_log!` only, no "hub disconnected", a watching hub keeps its role). A matched hub hotkey sends `{"event":"hotkey","function"}` only to the hub (`IpcServer::send_to_hub`); with no hub, `err_log!("hotkey <combo>: no hub connected")` and the key is swallowed, even when other watchers (the panel) exist. `err_log!("hub disconnected")` only when the hub closes. A press whose base keysym matches nothing is looked up again with `KeysymHandle::modified_sym()` when it differs, so `Ctrl+Shift+exclam` (as the lister's recorder writes it) matches Ctrl+Shift+1, and keypad keys with Num Lock alike. `compositor.json` unknown keys are logged with `err_log!` (the other keys still apply). Two hub entries with the same combo: the first wins, the error `hotkey <combo>: <function> duplicates <first function>`. A swallowed repeat press is traced with `debug_log!`. Harness: `Compositor::start` removes `OPUSDM_COMPOSITOR_CONFIG` from the child env and captures the compositor stderr (echoed, `wait_log` / `log_count`); `ipc_hub(&comp, table)` sends `hotkeys/set` then `watch` on one connection.
  - Verified: crate tests (76 unit, 48 headless, 15 ipc + 1 ignored helper); the rewritten / new headless tests failed on the previous sources (4 of 5) and pass now: hub gets the event and a plain watcher does not, hub A -> crash -> "hub disconnected" + "no hub connected" + key swallowed (0 keys in the client) + the panel-like watcher gets nothing -> hub B gets the event, one-shot `hotkeys/set` never logs "hub disconnected", `Ctrl+Shift+exclam` matched by Ctrl+Shift+1; clippy `-D warnings`; headless + ipc 5 runs green. Nested on a private Xvfb :7 (foot, xdotool on :7 only): `terminal`, `run` and `excl` (Ctrl+Shift+1 bound as `Ctrl+Shift+exclam`) reached the hub, `echo hi` typed into foot intact, Ctrl+Alt+BackSpace exit 0; `~/.config/opusdm` checksums and `picom.conf` mtime unchanged.
  - NOT verified: the hub side (tasks 10 and 12), the panel's permanent watch (task 11), keypad keys with Num Lock and non-latin layouts (no test), a host that autorepeats a held hotkey.
- Feature (compositor stage 2 task 5): global hotkeys in `opusdm-compositor`. `State::keyboard_key` now runs a filter before the focused client (new `src/hotkeys.rs`): the keysym of a press is the layout independent `KeysymHandle::raw_latin_sym_or_raw_current_sym()` (base level, so Shift does not change it), named with `xkb::keysym_get_name`, and looked up with the Ctrl / Alt / Shift / Super state (Caps / Num Lock ignored) in the order Quit (when enabled), WindowMenu, the hub's hotkeys (`combo_matches`: exact modifiers, single letters case-insensitive, other names exact). A matched press is swallowed and its keycode recorded (`State::swallowed_keys`); the release (and a repeat press) of a recorded keycode is swallowed too, even when the modifiers were released first; modifiers always reach the client. Every unmatched key reaches the client as before. New `src/config.rs`: `compositor.json` (`$OPUSDM_COMPOSITOR_CONFIG`, else `config_dir()/compositor.json`, read only) with `window_menu` (`Alt+space`), `quit` (`Ctrl+Alt+BackSpace`), `quit_enabled` (`true`); missing keys take the default, a missing file is the defaults (`debug_log!`), a bad file the defaults (`err_log!`), a key that does not parse falls back to its default (`err_log!`). Loaded once in `setup_common`. New IPC method `hotkeys/set` `{"hotkeys":[{"key","function"}]}` replaces the hub table and replies `{"ok":true,"errors":[...]}` (bad keys, and `hotkey <combo>: <function> conflicts with the built-in <name>, the built-in wins`, both also `err_log!`). The connection that sent it is the hub (`IpcServer::hub`, only its id); when it closes: `err_log!("hub disconnected")`, the table stays. A matched hub hotkey sends `{"event":"hotkey","function":...}` to every `watch` connection; with none: `err_log!("hotkey <combo>: no hub connected")`, key swallowed. Quit sets the quit flag (exit 0); WindowMenu calls the stub `State::open_window_menu()` (`debug_log!`, task 8 replaces it). `debug_log!` traces for every matched hotkey, swallowed release, hub connect and disconnect. Harness: `IpcWatch::wait_for_hotkey`.
  - Verified: crate tests (71 unit, 46 headless, 15 ipc + 1 ignored helper), the 5 new headless tests (hub hotkey reaches the watcher and not the client, hub reconnect, quit binding exit 0, unbound key reaches the client, conflict with Alt+space reported and the built-in wins) and unit tests (combo matching, config parse, set_hub conflicts and bad keys, lookup order, swallowed release after the modifier, `hotkeys/set` shape errors); `cargo clippy --all-targets -D warnings`; headless + ipc suites 5 times in a row, all green. Nested backend on a private Xvfb :7 (private D-Bus, temp HOME, `--startup foot`, xdotool only on :7): a Python hub sent `hotkeys/set` then `watch` on the same connection; Ctrl+backslash and Ctrl+Shift+space arrived as `hotkey` events (`terminal`, `run`), Alt+space was reported as a conflict and ran the window menu stub, `echo hi` typed into foot shows with no stray backslash or space (screenshot), Ctrl+Alt+BackSpace ended the compositor with exit 0. `compositor.json` paths checked by hand on the headless binary: bad JSON (err_log, defaults), `{"quit_enabled": false}` (quit off), missing file (debug_log, defaults). `~/.config/opusdm` checksums and `picom.conf` mtime unchanged before and after.
  - NOT verified: the hub side (task 12 sends `hotkeys/set`); the real OpusDM session (no live update: the compositor is not the session compositor yet); key repeat from a host that sends repeated presses (a recorded keycode's repeat press is swallowed, not re-run); non-latin layouts (the latin fallback of `raw_latin_sym_or_raw_current_sym` is not exercised). A hub combo written with a shifted symbol name (`Shift+question`) will not match, because the base-level keysym (`slash`) is used, as the brief asks; the VT-switch safety bindings are left for stage 4.
- Feature (compositor stage 2 task 4): `opusdm-compositor` serves ext-workspace (`ext_workspace_manager_v1` version 1, new `src/protocols/workspace.rs`), which the OpusDM pager already reads (`crates/wayland/src/client.rs`). Every bound manager gets one workspace group (`capabilities` 0, `output_enter` for every `wl_output` the client binds, also one bound later) and one workspace per desktop: `id` `"opusdm-desktop-<index>"`, `name` `"1"`..`"N"`, `coordinates` `[index]` (one u32 in the wire's native byte order, as the client parses it: little-endian on x86_64), `capabilities` `activate`, `state` `active` on the current desktop, then `done`. `activate` is recorded and applied on the manager's `commit` (`switch_desktop`; an error is logged with `err_log!`); `deactivate`, `assign`, `remove`, group `create_workspace` and requests on removed workspaces are ignored with `debug_log!`. `State::workspaces_update` runs at the top of `notify_watchers` (so after every desktop switch, count change, activate of a window on another desktop, client dispatch, IPC request and frame; one comparison per manager when nothing changed): new desktops get `workspace` + fields + `workspace_enter`, removed desktops get `workspace_leave` then `removed` (the plan said only `removed`; the protocol requires a workspace to leave its group before `removed`), the `active` state follows the current desktop, `done` closes each batch. Managers, groups and workspaces leave `State::workspaces` on `stop` (`finished`), on destroy and when the client dies (wayland-server `destroyed`); dead resources are also dropped on every update. New `debug/workspaces` IPC request (headless only) returns the manager / workspace counts for the leak test. Names that differ from the plan: no `wayland-protocols` server line in `[dependencies]`: the server side comes from Smithay's re-export (`smithay::reexports::wayland_protocols`, the same 0.32.13 crate, built by Smithay with `staging`), so `cargo tree -d` shows no second `wayland-server` / `wayland-protocols`; the dev-dependency `wayland-protocols` (client side of the harness) gets the `staging` feature. Harness: `TestClient::workspaces()` (`TestWorkspace`: `name`, `active`, `coordinates`), `activate_workspace(index)`, `workspace_groups()`. `crates/wayland/src/model.rs`: unit test `single_coordinate_workspaces_sort_in_index_order` pins `pager_order` for one coordinate per workspace (no client code change). Verified: `cargo test -p opusdm-compositor` (57 unit + 41 headless, 7 new, incl. `a_workspace_client_that_dies_leaves_no_workspaces_behind`, checked against a mutation without any cleanup: (2, 8) instead of (1, 4), fails; + 15 IPC), `cargo test -p opusdm-wayland` (28), clippy `-D warnings` for `opusdm-compositor`, headless + IPC suites 5 times; by hand on the headless backend (own temp runtime dir and HOME, no display): the real `opusdm-wayland` client (`cargo run -p opusdm-wayland --example watch switch`) listed workspaces 1-4 with coordinates 0-3 and 1 active, its `switch_workspace` moved to "2" (`desktops/list` current 1), after `desktops/set-count` 6 a new client listed 1-6, and `debug/workspaces` was 0 / 0 after the clients exited; `~/.config/opusdm` sha256 (16 files) and picom.conf mtime unchanged. NOT verified: the GTK panel pager was not run under the compositor; nothing run in the nested backend; `stop` / `finished`, a group destroyed by the client, a `wl_output` bound after the manager and the `err_log!` path of a failing `switch_desktop` have no test (code review only). Note: `cargo clippy -p opusdm-wayland -- -D warnings` fails with the current toolchain on three `chunks_exact` lines (`model.rs` 63 and 72, `screencopy.rs` 164) that were already there before this task (same 3 errors at the previous commit); not touched here (rule 1).
- Feature (compositor stage 2 task 3): `opusdm-compositor` serves wlr-foreign-toplevel-management (`zwlr_foreign_toplevel_manager_v1` version 3, new `src/protocols/foreign_toplevel.rs`), which the panel tasklist, the fullscreen detection and the tray start already read (`crates/wayland/src/client.rs`). Every bound manager gets one handle per window the IPC lists (`window_views`: every mapped toplevel, shown or hidden), with `title`, `app_id`, `output_enter` (every `wl_output` the client bound, also one bound later), `state` (maximized 0, minimized 1, activated 2, fullscreen 3 for handles of version 2+) and `done`; `closed` when the window is destroyed or unmapped by a null buffer (a remap announces a new handle). The handles follow the same `window_views` snapshot as the IPC watchers: `notify_watchers` now also runs `foreign_toplevels_apply` (after every dispatch, IPC request, wm operation and frame), and the xdg map / unmap / destroy / maximize / fullscreen / title / app id points call it at once (so a client roundtrip sees the change). Requests: `activate` and `unset_minimized` -> `activate_window` (as Openbox), `set_minimized` -> `set_minimized`, `close` -> `close_window`, `set_/unset_maximized` and `set_/unset_fullscreen` -> the stage 1 xdg request paths, `set_rectangle` ignored with `debug_log!`; a request on a closed handle or for a gone window is ignored with `debug_log!`. Handles and managers leave `State::foreign_toplevels` on closed / stop / destroy and when the client dies (wayland-server `destroyed`), dead ones are also dropped on every apply. New `debug/foreign-toplevels` IPC request (headless only, like `debug/key`) returns the manager / handle counts for the leak test. Names that differ from the plan: `State::foreign_toplevels` is a `ForeignToplevels` (managers + handles with what each was last told) instead of `Vec<(WindowId, handle)>`; no `wayland-protocols-wlr` line in `Cargo.toml`: the server side comes from Smithay's re-export (`smithay::reexports::wayland_protocols_wlr`, same 0.3.12 crate, enabled by `wayland_frontend`), so no second copy can disagree with Smithay's `wayland-server`. Harness: `TestClient` binds the manager (`toplevels()`, `activate`, `minimize`, `ForeignToplevel`). Verified: `cargo test -p opusdm-compositor` (56 unit, 1 new + 34 headless + 15 IPC; 7 new headless tests incl. `a_foreign_toplevel_client_that_dies_leaves_no_handles_behind`), clippy `-D warnings`, headless + IPC suites 5 times; by hand on the headless backend (own temp runtime dir, no display): foot plus the real `opusdm-wayland` client (`cargo run -p opusdm-wayland --example watch`) listed `FootTest` / `foot-test` on `HEADLESS-1`, saw minimized then activated after IPC minimize / activate, saw the list empty after `window/close`, and the counts went back to 0 / 0 when the example exited. NOT verified: the GTK panel tasklist, fullscreen detection and tray start were not run under the compositor; nothing run in the nested backend; `stop` / `finished`, `output_enter` for a `wl_output` bound after the handle and `set_/unset_fullscreen` from a handle have no test (covered by code review only).
- Fix (compositor stage 2 task 2, review): (1) new IPC test `closed_connections_give_back_their_fds_and_the_server_keeps_answering`: 150 requests, 60 garbage connections and a dropped watcher (more than `IPC_MAX_CONNECTIONS`), then the server still answers and `/proc/<pid>/fd` is back to its baseline (harness `Compositor::open_fds`); checked against a mutation where the read source never removes itself: 225 fds vs 13, test fails. (2) A peer EOF now shuts the connection socket down too (`_shutdown`), as the task 2 commit body said, so a pending write source wakes up and removes itself. (3) The task 2 entry's half-close note is corrected (see above). (4) The client pid is read once from the socket credentials when the toplevel is created (`WindowInfo::pid`, `state::client_pid`, failure or pid 0 -> `None` with `err_log!`, shown as `null`); `window_views` and `window/kill` use it, so `notify_watchers` no longer does one `getsockopt(SO_PEERCRED)` per window per dispatch / tick. (5) `window_close_asks_the_client_to_close` now waits for `xdg_toplevel.close` on the client (harness `wait_close_request`); fails with `send_close` removed (checked). Verified: `cargo test -p opusdm-compositor` (55 unit + 27 headless + 15 IPC), clippy `-D warnings`, all suites 5 times. NOT verified: the pid-0 path (a client in another pid namespace) has no test; nothing rerun in the nested backend for this fix.
- Feature (compositor stage 2 task 2): private IPC of `opusdm-compositor`. Socket `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/opusdm-compositor-<WAYLAND_DISPLAY>.sock` (a stale file is removed before bind, the file is removed when the loop ends: `Drop` of `IpcServer`, so session/quit, SIGTERM / SIGINT, host window close and loop errors all remove it), printed as a second stdout line `OPUSDM_COMPOSITOR_SOCKET=<path>` and exported to the `--startup` children as `OPUSDM_COMPOSITOR_SOCKET`. Wayfire framing (`src/ipc/frame.rs`), requests `windows/list`, `desktops/list`, `window/activate|minimize|close|kill|move-to-desktop`, `desktops/switch|set-count`, `session/quit`, `watch` (events `window-added`, `window-changed`, `window-removed`, `desktops-changed`, from a diff of the last snapshot in `State::notify_watchers`, called after every wm operation, after each client dispatch, after each IPC request batch and on each frame tick) and `debug/key` (only with `--headless`; modifiers, key press + release through `State::keyboard_key`, keycodes looked up in the active xkb layout). Non-blocking calloop sources (listener, one read source per connection, one write source only while bytes are pending), `IPC_MAX_MESSAGE` 1 MiB, `IPC_MAX_PENDING` 4 MiB, `IPC_MAX_CONNECTIONS` 64 (extra, not in the plan): a connection over a limit or with a protocol error (bad length, body not a JSON object with a string `method`) is dropped with `err_log!`; every refusal is an `{"error"}` reply plus `err_log!`. `window/kill` sends SIGKILL to the client pid from the socket credentials, refused for pid <= 1 and the compositor's own pid. `windows/list` and the events leave out toplevels that never mapped or were unmapped by a null buffer (a remap is a new `window-added` with the same id). The `#[allow(dead_code)]` of the Task 1 wm functions are gone. New deps of the crate: `serde`, `serde_json`, `libc` (workspace, already in Cargo.lock). Harness: `Compositor::ipc_socket`, `tests/common/ipc.rs` (`ipc_request`, `ipc_send_raw`, `ipc_watch`, `IpcWatch::next_event|wait_for|wait_for_event`), `TestClient::connect_to`, `wait_keys` / `keys_received` (non-modifier keys only, R5). Verified: `cargo test -p opusdm-compositor` (55 unit + 27 headless + 14 IPC integration, including desktop switching with two real windows: the window moved away loses the keyboard and gets it back on switch, a real SIGKILL of a helper client process spawned by the test, the socket file gone after session/quit and after SIGTERM), clippy `-D warnings`, all suites 5 times; nested backend on Xvfb :7 (private D-Bus, temp HOME, `--startup foot`): list (app_id foot, pid), minimize, move to desktop 2, activate (switches to 2), switch back, `debug/key` refused, kill of foot, session/quit exit 0, socket removed; `~/.config/opusdm` sha256 (16 files) and picom.conf mtime unchanged. NOT verified: no hub uses the IPC yet (tasks 3+); the `IPC_MAX_PENDING` drop is covered by a unit test only (no integration test floods a watcher); a client that half-closes its socket (SHUT_WR) after a request still gets its reply (replies are written at once), only the part of a reply larger than the socket buffer is lost when EOF drops the connection; `window-changed` for title / maximize changes driven by real apps was not watched in the nested run; nothing run with Xephyr (it needs the live :0 as host) or in the owner session. Note: `test_input` is set by `headless::run` on `State`, `args.rs` is unchanged.
- Fix (compositor stage 2 task 1, review): hiding a window (minimize, other desktop) while one of its menus held a popup grab left the keyboard on the hidden window (Smithay's popup keyboard grab ignores `set_focus` and gives the keyboard back to the grab root when the popup goes). New `State::dismiss_popups_and_grabs(root)` (`src/wm/mod.rs`, meant to be reused for exclusive layers in task 6): dismisses the popups of `root`, ends the keyboard and pointer grabs whose start focus is `root` (`debug_log!`), and moves a keyboard left on a dismissed popup to `root`; `sync_space` then gives it to the topmost shown window. Harness helpers `map_popup_with_grab` and `popup_done`; headless test `minimizing_a_window_with_an_open_menu_gives_the_keyboard_to_the_shown_window` (failed before the fix). Verified: `cargo test -p opusdm-compositor` (39 unit + 27 headless), clippy `-D warnings`, headless suite 5 times. NOT verified: the pointer side of the grab end is not asserted by a test (the harness has no pointer); nothing run in the nested backend or a sandbox.
- Compositor stage 2 task 1 (`crates/compositor`): window table with stable ids, virtual desktops and minimize. New `src/wm/mod.rs` (`WindowId`, ids from 1 never reused; `HIDDEN_FRAME_INTERVAL` = 1 s; `State::window_by_id`, `is_shown`, `sync_space`, `activate_window`, `set_minimized`, `move_to_desktop`, `switch_desktop`, `set_desktop_count`, `close_window`, every one `Err` on an unknown id) and `src/wm/desktops.rs` (`Desktops`, count clamped to 1..=16). `WindowInfo` now owns the `Window` of every toplevel plus `id`, `desktop`, `minimized`; `State` has `stack` (stacking order of every toplevel) and `desktops`; the space holds only the shown windows (buffer, not minimized, current desktop). `xdg_toplevel.set_minimized` now minimizes (stage 1 ignored it): the window leaves the space, its popups are dismissed, the keyboard goes to the topmost shown window (an exclusive layer keeps it); unminimize only through activation. Hidden windows with a buffer get frame callbacks every second. A window maps (and maps again after a null buffer) on the current desktop, not minimized. A hidden window that commits a null buffer is unmapped like a shown one (it gets a fresh initial configure). The desktop count comes from `DesktopSettings.virtual_screens` at startup (`setup_common`). The test harness now runs the compositor with `HOME` and `XDG_CONFIG_HOME` in its temp dir (the owner's `~/.config/opusdm` is never read). The stage 1 test `minimize_is_ignored_...` is replaced by `a_minimized_window_loses_the_keyboard_and_still_gets_frames`; new headless tests for a minimized window whose client dies and a minimized window unmapped by a null buffer; unit tests for `Desktops` and the `State` operations. Verified: `cargo test -p opusdm-compositor` (39 unit + 26 headless), `cargo clippy --all-targets -D warnings`, headless suite 5 times in a row. NOT verified: nothing run in the nested backend or in a sandbox (no tasklist / IPC yet to activate, switch desktops or unminimize; `activate_window`, `move_to_desktop`, `switch_desktop`, `set_desktop_count` and `close_window` are only unit-tested without windows, their first caller is task 2); popup dismissal of a hidden window and a minimize while a popup grab holds the keyboard are not exercised by a test. No Smithay 0.7.0 name differed from the plan.
- Plan (branch `opus-wayland`): `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-25-compositor-stage2.md`, stage 2 of the native compositor (window ids, desktops and minimize; private IPC; wlr-foreign-toplevel; ext-workspace; global hotkeys with the quit and window menu bindings; exclusive layer closing open menus; xdg-activation; compositor-drawn window menu with OpusDMTheme colours; `opusdm_wayland::native`; panel and hub native branches; sandbox config copy and hotkey table; exit check). Minimize, the window menu, xdg-activation, the popup-grab fix and the sandbox hotkey table were added to stage 2 by the owner. No code change. NOT verified: nothing built or run.
- Fix (sandbox, owner request, re-review): `run-x11.sh` now unsets the same `LIVE_SESSION_VARS` as `run-native.sh` / `run-wayland.sh` (`OPUSDM_PICOM_BASE OPUSDM_HUB_SOCK OPUSDM_DOCK_SOCK OPUSDM_APPLETS_SOCK OPUSDM_LABWC_RC`; the socket overrides replace `XDG_RUNTIME_DIR` in `crates/ipc`, so a sandbox hub would have talked to the live hub / dock / applets); a comment in each script says the three lists must stay in sync. `run-wayland.sh`: `own_pgid` is read with `|| true` and an empty one skips the group kill (with the stderr note), and the header comment describes the current design (compositor in the background under `set -m`). Verified in `Xephyr :7` (display `:8`): `run-x11.sh` launched with all five variables set to fake values: none of them in the sandbox hub's `/proc/<pid>/environ`; after `kill <script pid>` nothing left and no `/tmp/opusdm-xephyr*` dir. NOT verified: `run-wayland.sh` was only syntax checked after this change (comment, `|| true`, the extra `-n` test). Known, not fixed: TERM / Ctrl+C during the foreground `cargo build` of the sandbox scripts no longer stops cargo (the inner run has no signal handler until the compositor starts).
- Fix (sandbox, owner request): `run-x11.sh` keeps its picom away from the live one. Before, it exported `OPUSDM_PICOM_BASE` to the sandbox hub, which then rewrote `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/opusdm/picom.conf` (the owner's live `~/.config/opusdm/picom.conf` without `--config-dir`) and ran `pkill -USR1 -u <uid> -x picom`, reloading the live picom too. Now the script unsets `OPUSDM_PICOM_BASE` (a live session exports it) and starts the sandbox picom directly on the sandbox's own `picom.conf` (`PICOM_CONFIG`); with no base the hub's `compositor::apply` does nothing. Cost: transparency changes made in Settings inside the sandbox no longer apply live there (noted in the script header). No Rust change. Verified in `Xephyr :7` (sandbox display `:8`), WITHOUT `--config-dir`, with `OPUSDM_PICOM_BASE=/opt/opusdm/config/picom.conf` set in the environment of the run: the sandbox picom ran as `picom --config .../opusdm/picom.conf` (the repo file), the sandbox hub environment had no `OPUSDM_PICOM_BASE`, the live picom kept its PID (1986), `~/.config/opusdm/picom.conf` kept its mtime (08:01:11), no new telegram / megasync, nothing left after `kill`. NOT verified: changing transparency inside the sandbox (no Settings click was made), `--debug`.
- Fix (sandbox, owner request, review of the D-Bus wrappers): in `run-wayland.sh` and `run-x11.sh` the outer run no longer sends TERM to the whole group of `dbus-run-session` (it died at once, the outer run returned its status while the inner run was still stopping labwc / the hub and removing temp dirs). The inner run writes its pid into the marker file; the outer run passes TERM / INT / HUP to that pid only (the whole group only before the inner run has started), waits until `dbus-run-session` is gone and returns its status (as `run-native.sh`); `run-x11.sh` then ends what is left of the group (the hub's tools). `run-wayland.sh`: the pgid read from `autostart.pgid` is used only when it is a number > 1 and not the script's own group (otherwise logged on stderr and skipped), and the TERM / INT / HUP trap that points to the function-local pid is cleared before `_run_compositor` returns. Verified in `Xephyr :7`: `run-wayland.sh` (labwc), `kill <outer pid>`: the outer run returned status 0 (the inner run's) and right after nothing of the run was left and `/tmp/opusdm-wayland-*` / `/tmp/opusdm-labwc-*` were gone; `run-x11.sh` (display `:8` inside `:7`): after `kill <outer pid>` no process and no `/tmp/opusdm-xephyr*` dir left, status 1: that is the inner run's own status, set by the existing `_cleanup` EXIT trap whose last test (`[ -n "$CONFIG_DIR" ] && ...`) fails when no `--config-dir` is given (not changed here). NOT verified: `--wayfire`, `--headless`, HUP / INT on these two scripts after this change, the bad-pgid branch (not provoked).
- Fix (sandbox, owner request): `run-native.sh` ends the sandbox on SIGHUP too. The outer D-Bus wrapper and the inner run trap `TERM INT HUP` (before, closing the terminal sent HUP only to the outer run, which died and left the compositor, hub and private bus running, because `set -m` gives the sandbox its own process group), and the trap is now set before the background job starts (an empty pid is skipped), so a signal between `&` and `trap` cannot orphan it. Verified in an isolated `Xephyr :7`: `kill -HUP <script pid>` leaves no compositor / hub / notify / keyring-watch / dock process and no `/tmp/opusdm-native*` dir. NOT verified: Ctrl+C (INT) from a terminal, a signal in the tiny window before the trap (reordered, not provoked).
- Fix (sandbox, owner request): `run-x11.sh` always runs on a private D-Bus session, as `run-native.sh` / `run-wayland.sh`: it runs itself again under `dbus-run-session` (marker `OPUSDM_SANDBOX_BUS`, a temp file `/tmp/opusdm-xephyr-bus-*`), exits with a message when `dbus-run-session` is missing, and the outer run passes TERM / INT / HUP to the process group of `dbus-run-session` (trap set before the job starts) and returns the inner run's status. The wrapper sits before the argument parsing, so the inner run gets the arguments unchanged; everything else (own `_cleanup` trap, `--no-startup-apps`, picom handling and its `OPUSDM_PICOM_BASE`, `--config-dir`, `--debug`) is untouched. Also: the hub's runtime dir (`/tmp/opusdm-xephyr-*`, `HUB_RUNTIME_DIR`) is now removed in `_cleanup` (guarded against an empty variable); Startup Apps were already skipped (`--no-startup-apps`), unchanged. Verified (run inside `Xephyr :7`, sandbox display `:8`, with `--config-dir` on a scratch folder): the hub's `DBUS_SESSION_BUS_ADDRESS` is a private `/tmp/dbus-*` bus, not the live `/run/user/1000/bus`; no new telegram / megasync; `kill <script pid>` leaves no process of the run (Xephyr :8, openbox, picom, hub, notify, applets, keyring-watch, dock) and no `/tmp/opusdm-xephyr*` dir; the live picom and `~/.config/opusdm/picom.conf` (mtime 08:01:11) untouched. NOT verified: HUP and INT on `run-x11.sh`, `--debug`, `--grid-debug`, a run without `--config-dir`. Known, not fixed: without `--config-dir` the sandbox's picom is started with `~/.config/opusdm/picom.conf`, the live file (`PICOM_USER_CONF` falls back to `$HOME/.config`), and the hub rewrites its opacity keys: use `--config-dir` for a sandbox. A run without `--config-dir` was deliberately not run.
- Fix (sandbox, owner request): `run-wayland.sh` (1) starts the sandbox hub with `--no-startup-apps` (as `run-native.sh` / `run-x11.sh`): it no longer starts the owner's Startup Apps a second time; (2) removes the hub's runtime dir (`/tmp/opusdm-wayland-*`) with `CONFIG_DIR` in the one EXIT trap of the inner run (set before the first `mktemp`, guarded against empty variables; the outer run keeps its own trap for the D-Bus marker, a different process); (3) `opusdm-keyring-watch` no longer outlives the run: labwc / Wayfire run in the background under `set -m` (`_run_compositor`, as `run-native.sh`), TERM / INT / HUP are forwarded to them, and after they exit the compositor's process group and the group of the autostart script are ended with TERM (labwc starts the autostart in a session of its own, so the hub and its tools are not in the compositor's group: the autostart records its group in `$CONFIG_DIR/autostart.pgid`); no pkill by name. The outer D-Bus wrapper traps TERM INT HUP and sets the trap before the job starts (closing the terminal sent SIGHUP only to the outer run, now in a group of its own). Verified in an isolated `Xephyr :7` (labwc mode, `1440x900`, never `:0`): hub started with `--no-startup-apps`, no new telegram / megasync process, `kill <script pid>` (TERM) and `kill -HUP <script pid>` end labwc, hub, notify, keyring-watch and dock, and `/tmp/opusdm-wayland-*` / `/tmp/opusdm-labwc-*` are gone (before the group fix keyring-watch stayed behind). NOT verified: `--wayfire`, `--headless`, `--debug`, `--grid-debug` (code path unchanged except the `_run_compositor` wrapper), Ctrl+C (INT) from a terminal, Lister / hotkeys / hub checks of rule 4 (no Rust change).
- Fix (compositor, final stage 1 review, Important 2, owner request): a layer surface hidden with a null buffer while keeping its role (the wlr-layer-shell unmap) was never treated as unmapped: it kept its exclusive zone (maximized windows did not grow back), kept the keyboard as an OnDemand holder, and since `initial_configure_sent` stayed true it got no configure on its new initial commit, so a protocol-correct client waited forever. `layer::handle_commit` now hides a layer that showed a buffer and commits a null one, as a toplevel is unmapped: out of the layer map, parked in `State::hidden_layers`, `LayerSurfaceData.initial_configure_sent` reset, keyboard released (`_release_keyboard`), `usable_area_changed`; its next commit puts it back in the layer map and gets a fresh initial configure, its buffer maps it (and an exclusive keyboard layer takes the keyboard again). A layer is "shown" after its first buffer commit (a mark in its user data), so the bufferless commits before the first buffer change nothing. A hidden layer destroyed (role or client) leaves `hidden_layers` in `layer_destroyed`, with `refresh` dropping dead ones as a safety net. The GTK path (role destroyed, then a null buffer on the bare surface, dce2fef) is unchanged. Verified: new headless tests `a_panel_hidden_by_a_null_buffer_gives_back_its_zone_and_maps_again` (1920x1050 -> 1920x1080 on hide, configure 1920x30 on the new initial commit, back to 1920x1050), `an_exclusive_layer_hidden_by_a_null_buffer_gives_back_the_keyboard_and_takes_it_again` (both fail without the fix) and `a_hidden_exclusive_layer_whose_client_dies_leaves_nothing_behind`; all existing tests green; clippy `-D warnings`. NOT verified: a real client that hides a layer by null buffer (GTK destroys the role instead; no such OpusDM client is known), popups of a hidden layer (left as they are).
- Test harness (compositor, final stage 1 review, findings 4 and 5): `tests/common/mod.rs` keeps draining the compositor's stdout after the `WAYLAND_DISPLAY=` line (before, the pipe was closed and a `--startup` child that printed got EPIPE / SIGPIPE); `TestClient::roundtrip` now sends its own `wl_display.sync` and waits with `dispatch_until` at most 5 s, then panics with a clear message (before, a hung compositor hung `cargo test` forever); new helper `wait_configure`. New headless tests: `a_maximized_window_grows_when_the_panel_client_is_killed` (TOP panel with exclusive zone 30 owned by a second client, maximized window of the first at 1920x1050; the panel's client disconnects with the panel mapped; the window is reconfigured to 1920x1080 and keeps getting frames) and `a_hung_compositor_fails_the_roundtrip_instead_of_hanging` (SIGSTOP the compositor, `roundtrip` panics after 5 s; adds 5 s to the headless run). Verified: `cargo test -p opusdm-compositor`, clippy `-D warnings`, no stray compositor after the run. NOT verified: the stdout drain with a `--startup` child that prints (no harness test starts one; the change is a read loop to EOF).
- Fix (sandbox, owner request with finding 3): `run-wayland.sh` gets the same private D-Bus session as `run-native.sh` (re-exec under `dbus-run-session`, marker `OPUSDM_SANDBOX_BUS` naming a temp file the outer run creates, a message and exit 1 without `dbus-run-session`). Its inner run keeps labwc / Wayfire in the foreground with no trap, so the outer run passes TERM / INT to the whole process group of dbus-run-session (labwc / Wayfire included, as a terminal Ctrl+C reached them before) and exits with the status dbus-run-session hands back. labwc, Wayfire and `--headless` start the same way (read-through: everything after the wrapper is unchanged). `run-x11.sh` untouched. Verified: `bash -n`; one run of nested labwc in Xephyr :7 (`env -u WAYLAND_DISPLAY DISPLAY=:7 ./run-wayland.sh 1280x800`): the sandbox hub had `DBUS_SESSION_BUS_ADDRESS=unix:path=/tmp/dbus-...`, not the live `/run/user/1000/bus`; `kill <script pid>` ended labwc, the hub, the private bus and its portals; `picom.conf` mtime unchanged. Seen and NOT changed (pre-existing in `run-wayland.sh`, out of scope): `opusdm-keyring-watch` outlived the run (killed by hand), the hub runtime dir in /tmp is never removed, and the hub runs without `--no-startup-apps`, so it started the user's Startup Apps (a Telegram instance, which exited at once). NOT verified: Wayfire mode, `--headless`, Ctrl+C from a terminal.
- Fix (sandbox, final stage 1 review, finding 3): `run-native.sh` shared the live D-Bus session, so the sandbox hub could take `org.freedesktop.FileManager1`, the notification and SNI names from the live hub. It now always runs itself again under `dbus-run-session` (marker `OPUSDM_SANDBOX_BUS`, the path of a pid file the outer run creates; the inner run writes its pid there and unsets the marker, so nothing it starts inherits it); without `dbus-run-session` it exits with a message. dbus-run-session dies on TERM without passing it on (tested: it left its child script orphaned), so the outer run does not `exec`: it keeps dbus-run-session in a process group of its own, passes TERM / INT to the inner run (whose existing trap ends the compositor and its process group) and exits with the status dbus-run-session hands back. `docs/WAYLAND-PORT.md` usage updated. Verified: `bash -n`; one run in Xephyr :7 (`env -u WAYLAND_DISPLAY DISPLAY=:7 NATIVE_LOG=... ./run-native.sh --debug 1440x900`, no outer dbus-run-session): the sandbox hub had `DBUS_SESSION_BUS_ADDRESS=unix:path=/tmp/dbus-...` (live hub: `unix:path=/run/user/1000/bus`), the private bus owned its own `org.freedesktop.Notifications`; `kill <script pid>` ended the compositor, hub, notify, keyring-watch, dock, the private dbus-daemon and the portals / at-spi / gvfsd it had activated, and removed the temp dirs and the pid file; `~/.config/opusdm/picom.conf` mtime unchanged (08:01:11); a bad resolution exits 1 through the wrapper; no `dbus-run-session` on PATH exits 1 with the message. NOT verified: Ctrl+C from an interactive terminal (only `kill` was used), the exit status of a TERM-ended run (nohup background, not captured), any input inside the sandbox.
- Fix (compositor, final stage 1 review, finding 2): `child_env` (the environment of the `--startup` command, so of the hub in `run-native.sh` and of anything started inside the compositor) now also removes `OPUSDM_PICOM_BASE`, `OPUSDM_HUB_SOCK`, `OPUSDM_DOCK_SOCK`, `OPUSDM_APPLETS_SOCK` and `OPUSDM_LABWC_RC`, the live session overrides that `run-native.sh` already unsets (`LIVE_SESSION_VARS`); a comment in both places says the lists must stay the same and a unit test reads `run-native.sh` and compares them. `spawn_startup` builds its command through `_apply_child_env` (same behaviour). Verified: new unit tests `a_child_does_not_inherit_the_host_display_or_the_live_overrides` (a child given DISPLAY, OPUSDM_PICOM_BASE, OPUSDM_HUB_SOCK on its own `Command`, not on the test process, sees none of them and sees WAYLAND_DISPLAY=wayland-9), `spawn_startup_applies_the_child_environment` (spawns through `spawn_startup` itself; meaningful here because the test process runs in the live session with DISPLAY and OPUSDM_PICOM_BASE set) and `the_live_session_list_matches_run_native_sh`; all fail when OPUSDM_PICOM_BASE is taken out of `child_env`. NOT verified: a run of the compositor launched directly from a live session without `run-native.sh` (covered only by the unit tests).
- Fix (compositor, final stage 1 review, finding 1): a window's menu (xdg popup grab) took the keyboard from an exclusive keyboard layer (the Run window) and never gave it back: Smithay focuses the popup, then the popup's root window when the menu closes, while `exclusive_layer` still named the Run window. `XdgShellHandler::grab` now refuses the grab (`err_log!` + `popup_done`) when an exclusive keyboard layer is mapped and the popup's root is not that layer (a popup of the layer itself, nested or not, has the layer as root and keeps its grab). Verified: new headless tests `a_window_menu_does_not_take_the_keyboard_from_an_exclusive_layer` (fails without the fix: the grab was accepted) and `a_menu_of_the_exclusive_layer_gets_its_grab` (the layer's own menu gets the keyboard, and the keyboard is back on the layer after the menu is destroyed); Smithay 0.7 does not validate the grab serial, the harness sends the last `wl_keyboard.enter` serial. `cargo test -p opusdm-compositor`, clippy `-D warnings`. NOT verified: a real GTK menu over a real `opusdm-run` in the nested compositor (no pointer / keyboard input was driven).
- Fix (sandbox scripts, task 8 review): the `run-native.sh` sandbox hub reached the owner's live session. The live session exports `OPUSDM_PICOM_BASE=/opt/opusdm/config/picom.conf`; the sandbox hub inherited it, so `crates/hub/src/compositor.rs::apply` rewrote the real `~/.config/opusdm/picom.conf` (mtime 08:01:11, the start of check run 5; content identical) and ran `pkill -USR1 -u <uid> -x picom` against the live picom. `run-wayland.sh` had the same leak. Both scripts now `unset` `OPUSDM_PICOM_BASE`, `OPUSDM_HUB_SOCK`, `OPUSDM_DOCK_SOCK`, `OPUSDM_APPLETS_SOCK` and `OPUSDM_LABWC_RC` before starting anything (`run-wayland.sh` sets `OPUSDM_LABWC_RC` again for its own rc.xml, as before); without `OPUSDM_PICOM_BASE` `apply` returns at once (no write, no pkill). Also: `run-native.sh` runs the compositor in the background in a process group of its own (`set -m`), traps TERM / INT to `kill -TERM` it, waits for it and exits with its exit code, then sends TERM to what is left of its process group (`opusdm-keyring-watch` is not a Wayland client and outlived the hub in the first try); so `kill <script pid>` no longer leaves the compositor and the hub orphaned. Both scripts exit with a message when `--debug` is given and the log's folder does not exist (before, the redirect in the autostart failed and the hub never started, silently). Audit of other live session effects of a sandbox hub (not changed, the script cannot prevent them by environment): the hub writes the user's real `~/.config/opusdm` (settings, seeded hotkeys and file types, never overwriting) and makes `opusdm-lister.desktop` the folder handler in `~/.config/mimeapps.list` (only when it is not already); `opusdm-keyring-watch`'s Restart action (only on a user click in its tray menu) runs `pkill -u <uid> -x gnome-keyring-daemon` style kills and `systemctl --user`, which reach the live session; `opusdm-polkit` registers for the live logind session (refused while the live agent runs); `XDG_RUNTIME_DIR`-based sockets are already private (own runtime dir) and D-Bus is private only when the sandbox is started with `dbus-run-session` (documented). Verified (Xephyr :7, own D-Bus, xdotool / import on :7 only): the sandbox hub's environment has no `OPUSDM_PICOM_BASE` (from `/proc/<pid>/environ`), `~/.config/opusdm/picom.conf` mtime still 08:01:11 after the run and the live picom (same pid) still running; `kill <script pid>` ended the compositor, hub, notify, keyring-watch, dock, a Lister and its glycin loaders, every listed pid gone, both temp dirs removed, no `wayland-*` socket left; with an unreachable `DISPLAY` the script exits 1 like the compositor; `NATIVE_LOG=/nonexistent-dir/x.log ./run-native.sh --debug` exits 1 with the message before building; `bash -n` on both scripts. NOT verified: that the live picom got no SIGUSR1 (inferred: `apply` returns before `pkill` without the variable); `run-wayland.sh` was only syntax checked and read, labwc / Wayfire were not launched; Ctrl+C from a terminal (INT goes through the same trap, not exercised).
- Test (compositor, stage 1, task 8 review): two integration tests for a layer surface created again on a `wl_surface` whose layer role was destroyed (what GTK does when a hidden layer window is shown again): `a_layer_surface_created_again_on_the_same_surface_gets_its_own_size` (anchored TOP, asked 200x60, configured 200x60) and `a_layer_surface_created_again_does_not_inherit_the_roleless_anchors` (no `set_anchor`, width 0: a protocol error as on a fresh surface; the compositor keeps accepting clients). New test helpers `map_layer_again` and `layer_again_without_anchor`. Verified: with the `_clear_roleless_anchor` call commented out the second test fails and the first passes (a client's `set_anchor` replaces the whole anchor set, so only a client that never sends it can see the leftover anchors); with the code as committed `cargo test -p opusdm-compositor` 30 unit + 17 integration pass, clippy clean. No production code changed.
- Feature (compositor, stage 1, task 8): `run-native.sh [--debug] [resolution]`, the sandbox of the native compositor, modelled on `run-wayland.sh`: exits with a message when `cargo` is missing (and on a resolution that is not `WxH`), builds the workspace (`cargo build --release --workspace`), writes an `autostart` in a temp config dir (absolute `WAYLAND_DISPLAY`, `XDG_RUNTIME_DIR` switched to a fresh temp dir for the hub, `GDK_BACKEND` unset, `target/release` first on `PATH`, `OPUSDM_DEBUG=1` with `--debug`) and runs `target/release/opusdm-compositor --nested --size $RESOLUTION --startup "sh $CONFIG_DIR/autostart"`; a `trap` removes both temp dirs. Differences from the plan: the compositor is run as a child, not with `exec`, because after `exec` the shell is gone and its EXIT trap never removes the temp dirs (checked: after each SIGTERM of the sandbox no `/tmp/opusdm-native*` dir was left); the hub is started with `--no-startup-apps` (as `run-x11.sh`), otherwise the sandbox would start the owner's Startup Apps (Telegram, megasync) a second time; `NATIVE_LOG` overrides the debug log path (default `/ramdisk/opusdm.log`, as `run-wayland.sh`) and `NATIVE_RES` the default resolution (1920x1080). `docs/WAYLAND-PORT.md`: new section "Native compositor (opusdm-compositor)" (what stage 1 covers, how to run the sandbox, what is missing until stage 2). Stage 1 exit check, by hand, only inside an isolated `Xephyr :7` (1600x1000, compositor 1440x900) with its own D-Bus session (`dbus-run-session`), driven with `DISPLAY=:7 xdotool`, files only in a scratch folder, never on the live `:0`; the owner's `~/.config/opusdm` was checked unchanged afterwards (sha256 of every file before / after); five runs, the last one (run 5) with the final code; screenshots and logs in `.superpowers/sdd/2026-11-25-compositor-stage1/` (`r5-*.png` for run 5, `task-8-*.png` / `task-8-*-runN.log` for runs 1-4). Results: desktop (BACKGROUND) with its icons, panel (TOP) and dock (BOTTOM, on the right side as configured, below the windows) shown PASS (`r5-00-startup.png`); a maximized window respects the panel's exclusive zone PASS (log `Maximized to Rect { x: 0, y: 41, w: 1440, h: 859 }`, `r5-08-maximized.png`); right click on the desktop: the menu opens, inside the output (flipped up near the bottom-right corner), a click outside closes it PASS (`task-8-02`, `-03`, `r5-04`, `r5-07`); right click on a panel item (the quick app button; the clock and the bell have no right click menu): popover opens, a click outside closes it PASS (`r5-14`, `r5-15`); double click on a desktop folder opens a Lister there PASS (`r5-01`, log `navigate_to /home/fabio/Desktop/godot`); Lister: Enter in the path bar PASS, double click on a folder PASS, Backspace PASS, parent button PASS, Alt+Left / Alt+Right PASS (each with its `navigate_to` log line), F4 (terminal drawer) PASS (`r5-03`), F5 / F3 NOT CHECKED (xdotool in this Xephyr sends every F key with Alt held, `xinput test` shows keycode 64 pressed around 71; the Lister rightly ignores Alt+F5; the compositor forwards the modifiers it gets: `cat -v` in foot prints `^[[15;3~`; the Copy job was run with the toolbar button instead); hub: a second Lister (desktop menu, New Lister) PASS, SOURCE / DEST roles PASS (`RoleChanged` log, `r5-05`), a Copy job PASS (`file2.txt` copied into the scratch `dest`, `JobDone ... copied: 1`), a function run PASS (command palette, function "Next Desktop": `FunctionStepDone exit_code 0`, `FunctionDone`; switching desktops itself does nothing until stage 2); Run window: gets the keyboard at once PASS (typed without a click, `r5-11`, log `exclusive keyboard layer ... takes the keyboard`, Escape closes it and releases the keyboard), but "opened from the panel or the desktop menu" NOT CHECKED: neither has a Run entry (its only launcher is the Ctrl+Shift+space hotkey), so `opusdm-run` was started directly with the sandbox hub's environment; move by title bar PASS, Alt + drag PASS, Alt + right drag resize PASS, maximize / restore PASS (logs `move grab`, `resize grab ... final configure acked`, `Maximized` / `unMaximized`); fullscreen PASS with `foot --fullscreen` instead of a video in the player (no video file in the scratch folder): covers the panel (`r5-12`), leaving fullscreen NOT CHECKED; `kill -9` of a Lister: the compositor survives and the other Lister keeps working (Backspace navigates) PASS (`r5-13`); log: the run 5 compositor log has only the expected `linux-dmabuf not offered (the EGL device is a software renderer)` line and winit's `WARN error setting XSETTINGS` (Xephyr runs no XSETTINGS manager, harmless); the two compositor errors found in runs 1-2 were fixed (separate commits above: opusdm-notify killed with `invalid_size`, EGL `BAD_SURFACE`); the hub log lines come from the sandbox, not from the compositor: no Secret Service on the private bus (keyring-watch, its notification), polkit agent refused (the live session's agent owns the logind session), NetworkManager secret agent not authorized, audio tray `Access denied`, `wlr-randr` fails (no wlr-output-management in stage 1), global hotkeys not available, applets are X11 only, the dock socket not up yet at the first `SetHidden` (answered right after), GTK warnings (no Inhibit portal on the private bus, Vulkan `SURFACE_LOST` probes on llvmpipe, `gtk-modules` key). Global hotkeys are NOT part of stage 1 (stage 2): Ctrl+backslash, Ctrl+Shift+space, Ctrl+Print, Alt+1 / Alt+2 were not tested and do nothing in this sandbox. Also seen, not fixed: the desktop menu is a widget inside the desktop's BACKGROUND layer surface (a `GtkOverlay` in `crates/hub/src/desktop_menu.rs`), not an xdg popup, so a window covers it (`task-8-49`; the same design as on X11, not a compositor bug); in run 1 a first double click on a desktop folder (xdotool, 80 ms between clicks) opened a Lister at `$HOME` (a background double click), not reproduced at 120 ms; in run 2 one pointer motion after the Run window closed was not followed (the next one was) and once Shift stayed held in the compositor's keyboard state while X had no key down (cleared by a Shift press), cause not found, not reproduced in runs 3-5; one frame left a one pixel line at the right edge after a menu closed (`task-8-03`); a Lister that opens its terminal drawer grows wider than the output (client size). The sandbox's `dbus-run-session` also started `xdg-document-portal`, which mounted `/run/user/1000/doc` (no document portal of the live session was running); it was unmounted when the sandbox stopped. Workspace checks: `cargo test --workspace` with `DISPLAY=:0` fails in `opusdm-shot` (its GTK tests call `gtk::init` from several test threads: "Attempted to initialize GTK from two different threads" and a SIGSEGV; `crates/shot` and every locked dependency version are the same as at `9b64ac0`, so not caused by stage 1); without a display (`env -u DISPLAY -u WAYLAND_DISPLAY cargo test --workspace --no-fail-fast`) all 3077 tests pass (22 ignored). `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings` fails on lints of the installed clippy 0.1.98 in crates unchanged since `9b64ac0`: `opusdm-wayland` (3x `chunks_exact` with a constant size), `opusdm-screen` (`sort_by_key`), `opusdm-viewer` (complex type, `get(0)`), `opusdm-lister` (`Default` for `FilterBar`, `is_empty` for `SizeCache`); not fixed here (other crates are out of the stage 1 scope); crates that depend on them were not linted; `opusdm-compositor` is clean. NOT verified: anything on the live session or a real GPU; the player and a video in fullscreen; leaving fullscreen; F5 / F3 and the other Lister F keys; the Run window from a panel or menu entry (there is none); global hotkeys (stage 2).
- Fix (compositor, stage 1, task 8 check): the nested backend asked the EGL buffer age when the window surface was not current, and Smithay logged `ERROR ... [EGL] 0x300d (BAD_SURFACE) eglQuerySurface` once per presented frame (53 lines in a 10 minute sandbox run, from minute 4 on, `task-8-compositor-run2.log`). Smithay 0.7's `bind` does not make the window surface current (only rendering does), so the "bind once before asking" of task 7 did not help; an import or a frame with no damage can leave the context on no surface. Now the age is asked only when `egl_surface().is_current()`, otherwise it is 0 (full redraw), which is what the failed query returned before: same rendering, no error line.
